Wait for all 4 agents to complete before proceeding to collect_confirmations. The 4 agents run concurrently and each one's completion arrives as a message in this conversation when it finishes — do NOT issue a separate blocking call to wait for them.\n285\t\n286\t**Once all 4 agents have reported completion, read each agent's output file (single message with 4 Read calls):**\n287\t```\n288\tRead tool:\n289\t  file_path: \"{outputFile from that agent's async_launched result}\"\n290\t```\n291\t\n292\t> Allow up to `workflow.subagent_timeout` for the slowest agent to finish before treating it as failed. The timeout is configurable via `workflow.subagent_timeout` in `.planning/config.json` (milliseconds). Default: 300000 (5 minutes). Increase for large codebases or slower models.\n293\t\n294\tEach output file contains that agent's completion confirmation. It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Nhydst9N6k2M1NgELs4MkH","type":"tool_result","content":"1\texport type Theme = 'light' | 'dark'\n2\t\n3\texport const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'theme'\n4\t\n5\t/** The current theme, read from html[data-theme]. Only 'dark' counts as dark. */\n6\texport function readTheme(): Theme {\n7\t  return document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n8\t}\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * Switch the theme: the attribute is written first so the switch always works, then the\n12\t * choice is stored. When storage is blocked the choice lasts for the page session only.\n13\t */\n14\texport function applyTheme(theme: Theme): void {\n15\t  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n16\t  try {\n17\t    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, theme)\n18\t  } catch {\n19\t    // Storage blocked (private mode, policy): the in-memory choice still applies.\n20\t  }\n21\t}\n22\t\n23\tconst DARK_QUERY = '(prefers-color-scheme: dark)'\n24\t\n25\tfunction isTheme(value: unknown): value is Theme {\n26\t  return value === 'light' || value === 'dark'\n27\t}\n28\t\n29\tfunction storedTheme(): Theme | null {\n30\t  try {\n31\t    const value = localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)\n32\t    return isTheme(value) ? value : null\n33\t  } catch {\n34\t    return null\n35\t  }\n36\t}\n37\t\n38\tfunction systemTheme(): Theme {\n39\t  try {\n40\t    return window.matchMedia(DARK_QUERY).matches ? 'dark' : 'light'\n41\t  } catch {\n42\t    return 'light'\n43\t  }\n44\t}\n45\t\n46\t/** Write the attribute without storing anything, and only when it would change. */\n47\tfunction syncAttribute(theme: Theme): void {\n48\t  if (readTheme() !== theme || !document.documentElement.hasAttribute('data-theme')) {\n49\t    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n50\t  }\n51\t}\n52\t\n53\t/**\n54\t * Subscribe to theme changes made outside the toggle: another tab changing or clearing the\n55\t * stored choice, and the OS scheme changing while nothing is stored. Both are folded into\n56\t * html[data-theme], which stays the single source of truth, and `onChange` fires when that\n57\t * attribute changes for any reason. Returns the cleanup.\n58\t */\n59\texport function subscribeTheme(onChange: () => void): () => void {\n60\t  const observer = new MutationObserver(onChange)\n61\t  observer.observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['data-theme'] })\n62\t\n63\t  // `storage` fires in other tabs only. A null key means the whole store was cleared.\n64\t  const onStorage = (event: StorageEvent) => {\n65\t    if (event.key !== null && event.key !== THEME_STORAGE_KEY) return\n66\t    syncAttribute(storedTheme() ?? systemTheme())\n67\t  }\n68\t  window.addEventListener('storage', onStorage)\n69\t\n70\t  let mql: MediaQueryList | null = null\n71\t  try {\n72\t    mql = window.matchMedia(DARK_QUERY)\n73\t  } catch {\n74\t    // matchMedia missing or blocked: OS scheme changes are simply not followed.\n75\t  }\n76\t  const onSystemChange = () => {\n77\t    if (storedTheme() === null) syncAttribute(systemTheme())\n78\t  }\n79\t  mql?.addEventListener('change', onSystemChange)\n80\t\n81\t  return () => {\n82\t    observer.disconnect()\n83\t    window.removeEventListener('storage', onStorage)\n84\t    mql?.removeEventListener('change', onSystemChange)\n85\t  }\n86\t}\n87\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01PKUbVN6a7b7xcgA76bM27t","agent_id":"a95394deb18337775","session_id":"cb24716b-4117-4f14-8b25-6e3939021776","uuid":"49317c4d-e414-4b73-93a7-fe516bedb7b4","timestamp":"2026-11-20T07:01:07.572Z","tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01Nhydst9N6k2M1NgELs4MkH","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"rule"}}],"subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}

Lucide dropped brand icons. Prefer 3 hand-inlined SVGs if only 3-4 are needed (zero deps). |\n68\t\n69\t### Development Tools\n70\t\n71\t| Tool | Purpose | Notes |\n72\t|------|---------|-------|\n73\t| oxlint (scaffold default) | Lint | `create-vite` 9.2.1 ships `oxlint` in `npm run lint`. Keep it. |\n74\t| @types/node | `vite.config.ts` path alias | Needed for `node:path` / `import.meta.dirname`. |\n75\t| Prettier + `prettier-plugin-tailwindcss` | Class sorting | Optional, recommended once Tailwind classes grow. |\n76\t\n77\t## Verified Bundle Costs (measured, gzipped JS, Vite 8 production build)\n78\t\n79\t| Build | JS gz | Delta vs. baseline |\n80\t|-------|-------|--------------------|\n81\t| Baseline: React 19.3 + Tailwind CSS (2.2 kB gz CSS) | 68.6 kB | n/a |\n82\t| + react-spring only (`useSpring`, `useTrail`, `useSprings`) | 85.8 kB | **+17.2 kB** |\n83\t| + motion with TextEffect, Tilt, Magnetic, `MotionConfig` | 121.5 kB | **+52.9 kB** |\n84\t| + both | 138.6 kB | **+70.0 kB** |\n85\t| Lenis (ReactLenis) alone | 74.2 kB | +5.6 kB |\n86\t| OGL (minimal Renderer/Program/Mesh) | 81.4 kB | +12.8 kB |\n87\t| GSAP core + ScrollTrigger | 112.7 kB | +44.1 kB |\n88\t| three + @react-three/fiber (one box) | 309.5 kB | **+240.9 kB** |\n89\t\n90\t- Coexistence costs about +17 kB beyond motion. That is acceptable for a portfolio, but it is the marginal price of the \"both libraries\" decision, so use react-spring where it earns it.\n91\t- Full `motion` component is the heavy part (about 53 kB with presence/spring/gesture features). motion.dev's own figures: `motion` about 34 kB, `m` + `LazyMotion` about 4.6 kB initial (+15 kB `domAnimation`, +25 kB `domMax`), `useAnimate` mini about 2.3 kB. **Optional optimization (not tested):** because motion-primitives is copy-pasted source, you could swap `motion.x` for `m.x` and wrap the app in `LazyMotion`. `AnimatePresence`/`layout`/`drag` need `domMax`, which erodes the saving. Do this only if Lighthouse demands it.\n92\t- Code-split below-the-fold sections with `React.lazy` so the hero ships alone. Any 3D must be lazy-loaded (see Gaps).\n93\t- Not measured: runtime frame rate on a real phone. That belongs in an early performance spike.\n94\t\n95\t## The Four Named Tools\n96\t\n97\t### 1. motion-primitives (ibelick) : USE, as a copy-in source library. Confidence: MEDIUM-HIGH\n98\t\n99\t- Only deps are `motion`, `clsx`, `tailwind-merge`, `lucide-react`, plus a `@/lib/utils` `cn()` helper. **No shadcn init required.**\n100\t- Components are served as shadcn-registry JSON at `https://motion-primitives.com/c/<name>.json` (HTTP 200 verified).\n101\t- The official CLI is `npx motion-primitives@latest add <name>`. It works (tested) but the npm package is stale (0.1.0, March 2025) and **drops files into `./components/motion-primitives/` at the project root, not `src/`**. Either move them to `src/components/motion-primitives/` or skip the CLI and copy the registry JSON content. Decide once and document it.\n102\t- Needs the `@` alias in both `vite.config.ts` (`resolve.alias`) and `tsconfig.app.json` (`paths: { \"@/*\": [\"./src/*\"] }`). With TS 6/7, `paths` works **without** `baseUrl` (verified in the probe). shadcn's Vite doc still says to add `baseUrl`. You do not need it.\n103\t\n104\t### 2. react-spring : USE, scoped. Confidence: HIGH\n105\t\n106\t- Install `@react-spring/web@~10.1.2`. Import `useSpring, useSprings, useTrail, useChain, useTransition, animated, config` from `@react-spring/web`.\n107\t- Version note: `react-spring` (the umbrella, 10.0.4) is behind and v11.0.0-beta (beta.1 released 2026-10-01) is **removing it**, going ESM-only, and deprecating `useTransition` in favor of new `usePresence`/`usePresenceList`. Stay on 10.1.x stable. Avoid `useTransition` for new core UI (use motion's `AnimatePresence`) so the v11 migration stays trivial.\n108\t- Reduced motion is built in: `useReducedMotion` is exported from `@react-spring/web` (confirmed in the type definitions), and the global flag `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation: true })` snaps every spring to its target. Wire it once (snippet below).\n109\t- Dependencies are tiny and tree-shakable. Measured at about +17 kB gz for `useSpring/useTrail/useSprings`.\n110\t\n111\t### 3. realtimecolors.com : USE as a design tool, with caveats. Confidence: HIGH\n112\t\n113\t- The seed (`050315 / fbfbfe / 2f27ce / dedcff / 433bff`, Inter/Inter) is Text, Background, Primary, Secondary, Accent. Note `050315` on `fbfbfe` is the **light** theme pairing (dark text on light bg). The tool's light/dark toggle (Alt+T) lets you design both modes in one session. The project requires light and dark, so design both explicitly rather than inverting.\n114\t- It shows AA/AAA contrast traffic lights per role. Use them as the accessibility gate for text and interactive colors.\n115\t- Exports: CSS variables, Tailwind CSS, SCSS, and custom templates, in HEX/RGB/HSL/OKLAB/OKLCH. The tool lists a `tailwind.config.js` style Tailwind export and does not mention v4. **Export \"CSS\" with OKLCH and paste into `@theme`**, not the Tailwind-config export:\n116\t- Its type-scale presets (1.067 to 1.618) map to a `--text-*` ladder in `@theme`.\n117\t- The user wants alternatives explored: do that in the design phase (Realtime Colors' scheme generators: analogous/complementary/triadic, plus randomize with Space). Keep exactly 5 role tokens plus derived tints so swapping palettes later is a single-block edit.\n118\t\n119\t### 4. godly.design/posts : USE as inspiration only, not a dependency. Confidence: MEDIUM\n120\t\n121\t- `godly.design/posts` is a curated **feed of linked social posts** (mostly X links to designers' UI interaction clips), with category nav (Websites, Tools, Templates, etc.). No dates, tags, RSS, or API were visible. Treat it as a mood-board source: capture screenshots/links into the planning docs, then extract motion patterns (spring feel, press states, cursor effects) into a written reference list before coding. (Probe also noted `godly.website` currently 301-redirects to `recent.design`, so the brand's structure may have shifted. Rely on `godly.design/posts/` and verify when you actually browse.)\n122\t- No code or package implications.\n123\t\n124\t## Division of Responsibility (motion vs react-spring)\n125\t\n126\t| Concern | Owner | Why |\n127\t|---------|-------|-----|\n128\t| Mount/unmount, `AnimatePresence`, exit animations | **motion** | Mature, handles React 19 strict mode and transitions (13.1.1 and 13.4.6 fixes). |\n129\t| Layout and shared-element transitions (`layout`, `layoutId`, Morphing Dialog) | **motion** | react-spring has no equivalent. |\n130\t| Scroll-linked values (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, ScrollProgress, in-view reveals) | **motion** | Already bundled. 13.4.x moved offsets onto hardware-accelerated `ViewTimeline` where supported. |\n131\t| Text/number effects, stagger reveals, all motion-primitives prefabs | **motion** | They are written against it. |\n132\t| Simple drag, `whileTap`, `whileHover` | **motion** | Built in. No extra gesture lib. |\n133\t| Velocity-carrying physics: flick/toss, drag-release with momentum, rubber-band, pointer-chasing follower, squish-on-press toys | **react-spring** | `useSpring` with continuous retargeting is its sweet spot and gives the \"tactile\" signature. |\n134\t| Staggered chains of springs (`useTrail`, `useChain`) for playful sets of elements | **react-spring** | Concise API. |\n135\t| Anything in `@react-spring/three` | **react-spring** | Only if 3D is adopted. |\n136\t\n137\t## Tailwind v4 Notes\n138\t\n139\t- v4 `hover:` only applies under `@media (hover: hover)`, so hover styles never stick after a tap on phones. This matches the \"no desktop-only gestures\" requirement. Do **not** restore v3 behavior with `@custom-variant hover (&:hover)`.\n140\t- Browser floor: Safari 16.4+, Chrome 111+, Firefox 128+ (uses `@property`, `color-mix()`). Fine for this audience.\n141\t- No `tailwind.config.js`; `tw-animate-css` (the shadcn replacement for tailwindcss-animate) is not needed since motion handles animation.\n142\t\n143\t## Dark Mode (covered: Tailwind v4 + tiny script)\n144\t\n145\t## Font Loading\n146\t\n147\t- `import '@fontsource-variable/inter/wght.css'` in `main.tsx`. Verified: it declares `font-display: swap`, `font-weight: 100 900`, and per-script `unicode-range`, so a browser downloads only the **latin subset (48 kB woff2)** for English copy (latin-ext is 85 kB and only fetched if used). Vite emits all subsets to `dist/` (harmless unused files).\n148\t- `@fontsource-variable/inter/opsz.css` adds optical sizing (latin 73 kB). Worth it only if you want display-size tuning.\n149\t- **Preload is not automatic**: a bare `<link rel=\"preload\" href=\"@fontsource-variable/inter/files/...\">` in `index.html` is *not* rewritten by Vite (verified, stays unresolved). If you want a preload, use a small `transformIndexHtml` plugin or `import url from '...woff2?url'`. Skipping preload is acceptable with `font-display: swap`; revisit if Lighthouse flags it.\n150\t- If the design adds a display face from Realtime Colors, use the same Fontsource route (one variable family, latin only), and keep to 2 families total for mobile performance.\n151\t\n152\t## GitHub Pages Deploy\n153\t\n154\t## Alternatives Considered\n155\t\n156\t| Category | Recommended | Alternative | Why Not |\n157\t|----------|-------------|-------------|---------|\n158\t| Framework | Vite + React | Next.js / Astro | Next explicitly out of scope. Astro would be lighter on JS but the user wants React islands everywhere and these two React animation libs. |\n159\t| Animation split | motion for UI choreography + react-spring for signature physics | react-spring only | Loses `layoutId`/`AnimatePresence`/scroll and every motion-primitives component. |\n160\t| Animation split | (same) | motion only | Viable and smaller (saves 17 kB), but the user explicitly wants to try react-spring. Revisit if the spike shows react-spring adds nothing. |\n161\t| react-spring package | `@react-spring/web` | `react-spring` umbrella | Lagging, `deprecated` tag present, removed in v11 beta. |\n162\t| Installing primitives | Copy via registry JSON / CLI, then patch | Wait for an npm package | None exists. Source ownership is the model. |\n163\t| Styling | Tailwind v4 | Tailwind v3.4 | Only for pre-2023 browsers. motion-primitives and shadcn are on v4. |\n164\t| TypeScript | 6.0.x | 7.0.x | 7.0.2 compiled the probe cleanly, so it is available, but v6 is the scaffold default. Take 7 post-launch. |\n165\t| Dark mode | Tailwind data-attr + inline script | next-themes | Next-oriented abstraction we do not need. |\n166\t| Fonts | Fontsource self-hosted | Google Fonts `<link>` | Third-party request, render-blocking, privacy. |\n167\t\n168\t## What NOT to Use\n169\t\n170\t| Do not use | Why |\n171\t|------------|-----|\n172\t| `react-spring` umbrella package | Deprecated tag, removed in v11. Use `@react-spring/web`. |\n173\t| `react-spring` v11 beta | Beta, breaking `onRest`/`immediate` semantics. Wait for stable. |\n174\t| Raw `framer-motion` imports | motion 14 depends on `framer-motion` internally. Always import from `motion/react` so there is one copy. |\n175\t| motion-primitives `Cursor`, `Magnetic`, `Tilt`, `Spotlight` as *primary* interactions | Mouse-only. They are desktop garnish. |\n176\t| `shadcn init` | Unneeded; adds Radix/CVA you will not use. |\n177\t| `tailwind.config.js`, `@tailwind base/components/utilities` | v3 patterns. |\n178\t| `@use-gesture/react` by default | Last published March 2024, and motion already provides drag/tap/hover. Only add if a react-spring-driven drag cannot be done well otherwise. |\n179\t| `vite-plugin-image-optimizer` as the primary image tool | Compress-only (no srcset/format variants). Last publish Oct 2025. |\n180\t| Hard-coded absolute asset paths | Break when `base` changes. |\n181\t| Both libraries animating the same element/property | The one real coexistence hazard. |\n182\t\n183\t## Gaps: capabilities the four tools do NOT cover\n184\t\n185\t| Gap | Already covered? | Candidate A (default) | Candidate B | Notes |\n186\t|-----|------------------|-----------------------|-------------|-------|\n187\t| **Scroll-driven animation** | **Yes, by `motion`** (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, `scroll()`, motion-primitives ScrollProgress/InView) | motion `useScroll` (+0 kB) | GSAP ScrollTrigger (+44 kB gz measured). GSAP is now fully free incl. plugins per gsap.com. | Only choose GSAP for pinned/scrubbed multi-step timelines. Not needed for six sections. |\n188\t| **Smooth scroll** | No | **None (native scroll)** | Lenis 1.3.26 (+5.6 kB measured). `lenis/react` provides `ReactLenis`. | Lenis smoothing is **off on touch by default** (`syncTouch: false`), honors reduced motion by default (`respectReducedMotion: true`), but needs `anchors: true` for in-page nav and community reports it can feel unlike native scroll and conflict with focus/keyboard behavior. Smooth scroll is a taste item, not a requirement. Default: skip until the design wants it. |\n189\t| **3D / WebGL** | No | **None** (use CSS 3D via Tilt and react-spring transforms for tactility) | (a) OGL 1.0.11 for one hero shader/blob (+12.8 kB measured); (b) three 0.186 + @react-three/fiber 9.8.1 (+241 kB gz measured, peer `react >=19 <19.4`) with `@react-spring/three` | R3F is the one thing in this list that can blow the mobile budget. If 3D is wanted, lazy-load it, cap DPR, and pause off-screen. |\n190\t| **Gesture / drag** | **Yes, by `motion`** (`drag`, `dragConstraints`, `whileTap`, `Reorder`) | motion `drag` | `@use-gesture/react` 10.3.1 only for react-spring-driven drag | `@use-gesture` is stale (Mar 2024). react-spring has no pointer handling itself, so a react-spring drag needs it or hand-written pointer events. |\n191\t| **Page transitions** | **Partly** (`AnimatePresence`; new `AnimateView` in motion 13.4.0 for React 19.3 `ViewTransition`, 2026-09-14, so very new) | **Single page, no router.** Use MorphingDialog/MorphingPopover for project detail | If real routes are wanted: `react-router` (library mode) or `wouter` 3.13 with hash routing + motion `AnimatePresence` | GitHub Pages cannot rewrite SPA URLs. Real routes need a `404.html` copy of `index.html` or hash routing. Note `react-router` 8.4.0 requires Node `>=22.22` and React `>=19.2.7`. `react-router-dom` 7.18.4 still publishes. `AnimateView` is LOW confidence until proven. |\n192\t| **Image optimization** | No | **vite-imagetools 12.0.1** (verified on Vite 8) | Manual pre-resize with `sharp` script, commit outputs | Use `<picture>` with AVIF/WebP + explicit `width`/`height` + `loading=\"lazy\"` below the fold + `fetchpriority=\"high\"` on the hero/LCP image to prevent layout shift. |\n193\t| **Theming / dark mode** | Tailwind v4 | Tailwind `@custom-variant` + inline script (above) | `next-themes` | Covered, see Dark Mode. |\n194\t| **Icons (brand)** | lucide has none | 3-4 inline SVGs for GitHub/LinkedIn/email | `simple-icons` 16.x or `react-icons/si` 5.7 | Lucide for UI glyphs only. |\n195\t| **Physics \"toys\" (stack/pile of objects, rigid-body)** | No | None (react-spring + motion drag can fake most) | matter-js 0.20.0 (last release 2023) or Rapier via `@react-three/rapier` | Only if a falling-objects/gravity playground is part of the identity. Lazy-load. |\n196\t| **Lottie/Rive illustrations** | No | None | `@rive-app/react-canvas` 4.36 (interactive, state machines) | Only if designed assets exist. |\n197\t| **Analytics, forms, contact** | No | `mailto:` / static links | Formspree-style form post | No backend (out of scope). |\n198\t| **Testing** | Not requested | Vitest + Playwright (visual/mobile viewport + axe) | none | Out of the four-tool scope; suggest a mobile-viewport Lighthouse CI check in the deploy workflow. |\n199\t\n200\t## Installation\n201\t\n202\t# Scaffold (Node >=22.12 recommended)\n203\t\n204\t# Core\n205\t\n206\t# Dev\n207\t\n208\t# Then add motion-primitives components one at a time (patch type imports, move to src/)\n209\t\n210\t## Decisions needed from user\n211\t\n212\t## Risks to Carry Into Roadmap\n213\t\n214\t| Risk | Severity | Mitigation |\n215\t|------|----------|------------|\n216\t| motion 14.0.0 is 6 days old and motion releases very frequently (multiple minor versions per month) | Medium | Pin `~14.0.0`, commit lockfile, keep 12.43 / 13.5.1 as fallback versions, avoid `AnimateView` and other brand-new APIs. |\n217\t| motion-primitives is \"beta\", code is copy-in | Low | Treat as owned code. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MQL7z67WHCqDDariXCiDZi","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Architecture\n2\t\n3\tStatus: Accepted\n4\t\n5\tThis document describes how the portfolio site is structured: layers, import rules, folder layout, data flow, the content model, the design-token pipeline and the animation layer. It is written so the scaffold can be built directly from it. Decisions with a longer rationale live in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n6\t\n7\t## Overview\n8\t\n9\tThe site is a static, zero-backend, single-page React app served from GitHub Pages. There is no router, no global store and no data fetching. The architecture comes down to four seams:\n10\t\n11\t1. Typed content: all copy lives in TypeScript modules checked against one schema.\n12\t2. CSS-variable design tokens: five role colors per theme drive every surface.\n13\t3. An internal animation layer: the only place the two animation libraries are imported.\n14\t4. A build and deploy pipeline: Vite build, GitHub Actions, GitHub Pages.\n15\t\n16\tEverything else is conventional React composition with props flowing down.\n17\t\n18\t## Layers\n19\t\n20\tDependencies point down only. A layer may import from layers below it, never above.\n21\t\n22\t```text\n23\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n24\t| app/        main.tsx, App.tsx, Providers, Page.tsx                    |\n25\t|             Page.tsx is the ONLY file that imports content data       |\n26\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n27\t| sections/   Hero, About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education,     |\n28\t|             Contact                      (props in, JSX out)          |\n29\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n30\t| layout/     Container, Section, SiteHeader, MobileNav, Footer,        |\n31\t|             SkipLink                                                  |\n32\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n33\t| components/ ui/ (Button, Tag, Card, Heading, Logo, ThemeToggle, Icon) |\n34\t|             motion-primitives/ (vendored source, treated as owned)    |\n35\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n36\t| anim/       the internal animation layer: the only place that         |\n37\t|             imports motion/react and @react-spring/web               |\n38\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n39\t| hooks/  lib/                  | styles/ (tokens.css, base.css,       |\n40\t| (useActiveSection, cn, a11y)  |  index.css = tailwind + theme map)   |\n41\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n42\t| content/ (typed TS data)      assets/ (images, fonts via Fontsource) |\n43\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n44\tBuild and deploy: vite.config.ts, lint boundary rules, GitHub Actions\n45\t```\n46\t\n47\t## Import rules\n48\t\n49\tOnly src/anim/ and src/components/motion-primitives/ may import motion/react or @react-spring/web.\n50\t\n51\tOnly src/app/Page.tsx imports content data.\n52\t\n53\t| Importer | May import | May not import |\n54\t|----------|-----------|----------------|\n55\t| sections/* | layout, components/ui, the anim barrel (src/anim/index.ts), content types (not data) | motion/react, @react-spring/*, components/motion-primitives/* directly, content data |\n56\t| layout/*, components/ui/* | the anim barrel, lib, hooks | sections, content data |\n57\t| components/motion-primitives/* | motion/react, lib/cn | everything else (vendor code, kept out of lint autofix churn) |\n58\t| anim/* | motion/react, @react-spring/web, components/motion-primitives/*, the token mirror in styles | sections, layout |\n59\t| app/Page.tsx | content data, sections | any animation library or anim internals |\n60\t\n61\tThese rules are enforced by oxlint's `no-restricted-imports` rule with per-folder overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, covering dynamic `import()` and re-exports as well, so a violation fails CI rather than relying on review. Unit tests under `tests/unit` may import the libraries for assertions. A gesture library is not adopted up front. It would be added to the anim row only if a react-spring drag cannot be built well otherwise, after a React 19 compatibility check.\n62\t\n63\t## Folder structure\n64\t\n65\t```text\n66\trepo root\n67\t|-- public/                  URL-stable files only: favicon set, og image,\n68\t|                            resume.pdf (a phone-number-free copy), robots.txt\n69\t|-- index.html               meta and social tags, inline theme-init script,\n70\t|                            noscript fallback content\n71\t|-- vite.config.ts           base from env, Tailwind plugin, imagetools, \"@\" alias\n72\t|-- .oxlintrc.json           import-boundary rules (see Import rules)\n73\t|-- .github/workflows/       ci.yml, deploy.yml\n74\t`-- src/\n75\t    |-- main.tsx\n76\t    |-- app/                 App.tsx, Page.tsx, Providers.tsx\n77\t    |-- sections/            one folder per section (Hero, About, Experience,\n78\t    |                        Projects, Skills, Education, Contact)\n79\t    |-- layout/              Container, Section, SiteHeader, MobileNav,\n80\t    |                        Footer, SkipLink\n81\t    |-- components/\n82\t    |   |-- ui/              Button, Tag, Card, Heading, Logo, ThemeToggle, Icon\n83\t    |   `-- motion-primitives/   vendored, patched locally, logged in an ADR\n84\t    |-- anim/\n85\t    |   |-- index.ts         public barrel: the only animation import path\n86\t    |   |-- MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx\n87\t    |   |-- useMotionPreference.ts\n88\t    |   |-- tokens.ts        springs, durations, eases in both libraries' formats\n89\t    |   |-- declarative/     motion-owned wrappers (Reveal, Stagger, Presence,\n90\t    |   |                    TextReveal, ScrollProgress, Morph)\n91\t    |   `-- physics/         react-spring-owned wrappers (PressSpring and other\n92\t    |                        pointer-driven, velocity-carrying helpers)\n93\t    |-- content/\n94\t    |   |-- schema.ts        every type\n95\t    |   |-- profile.ts, experience.ts, projects.ts, skills.ts,\n96\t    |   |   education.ts, contacts.ts     one module per section\n97\t    |   |-- images.ts        image registry\n98\t    |   `-- index.ts         assembles siteContent with satisfies\n99\t    |-- hooks/               useActiveSection, useMediaQuery, useHoverCapable\n100\t    |-- lib/                 cn.ts, date formatting, a11y helpers\n101\t    |-- styles/\n102\t    |   |-- index.css        tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping\n103\t    |   |-- tokens.css       role tokens, derived tokens, light and dark blocks\n104\t    |   `-- base.css         resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts\n105\t    `-- assets/images/       hashed by Vite\n106\t```\n107\t\n108\tThe animation folder is named anim/ rather than motion/ so it is never confused with the motion package or its motion/react import path.\n109\t\n110\t## Data flow\n111\t\n112\tBuild time:\n113\t\n114\t```text\n115\tcontent/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> app/Page --props--> sections --props--> layout, ui\n116\tassets/*     --(imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\n117\t```\n118\t\n119\tRuntime, all client-side with no network use beyond same-origin assets:\n120\t\n121\t- Theme: an inline script in the document head reads localStorage, falls back to prefers-color-scheme, and sets the data-theme attribute on the html element before first paint. The theme provider in React only changes the attribute and storage on a user toggle.\n122\t- Motion preference: the OS reduced-motion setting (plus an optional site override) feeds one hook, useMotionPreference, which configures both animation libraries and the wrapper components. See Reduced motion.\n123\t- Scroll-spy: an IntersectionObserver over the section ids drives useActiveSection, which highlights the current section in the navigation.\n124\t- Pointer and touch input drive react-spring values written straight to the DOM, with no React re-render per frame.\n125\t- Project detail: a click or tap sets local state in the Projects section, which opens an overlay with focus handling.\n126\t\n127\tThere is no global state library. Three small contexts exist: theme, motion preference and, optionally, the active section. Everything else is props.\n128\t\n129\t## Content model\n130\t\n131\tAll types live in src/content/schema.ts:\n132\t\n133\t- Profile: name, tagline, bio paragraphs, optional quote, photo (ImageAsset), contacts.\n134\t- ContactLink: kind (email, github, linkedin or instagram), label, href, whether it is external.\n135\t- ExperienceEntry: id, role, org, optional org URL, optional logo, dates (DateRange), location, bullets, optional highlights and stack.\n136\t- Project: id, title, context kind (personal, employer, course or hackathon) with a label, dates, stack, bullets, relatedExperienceId, featured, optional links and image.\n137\t- EducationEntry: id, degree, school, location, dates, gpa, optional track.\n138\t- SkillGroup: id, label, items as a string array.\n139\t- DateRange: ISO start and end (or present) plus a display label in one normalized format.\n140\t- ImageAsset: src, alt, width, height, optional srcset.\n141\t\n142\tRules:\n143\t\n144\t- Content modules use satisfies against the schema so a missing field fails typecheck.\n145\t- Ordering lives in the data (array order or a priority field), never in components.\n146\t- Copy is taken verbatim from the reviewed content inventory; any edit is recorded there with before and after, not made silently in code.\n147\t- The featured flag decides what appears first on mobile, so \"key info in seconds\" is a data decision.\n148\t- Content tests check that ids are unique, related ids resolve, every image has alt text, every external link is https, and dates parse in descending order.\n149\t- A phone number is not a field anywhere in the model, and must never appear in site copy, metadata or the served resume.\n150\t\n151\t## Design tokens\n152\t\n153\t- Exactly five role tokens per theme: text, background, primary, secondary, accent. Light and dark are authored separately, not inverted.\n154\t- Derived tokens (surface, border, muted text, link, focus ring and so on) are computed from the five roles with color-mix(in oklch, ...). A palette swap therefore edits one block and every derived value follows.\n155\t- The theme is selected by the data-theme attribute on the html element.\n156\t- Tailwind v4 is configured CSS-first: a custom dark variant is bound to [data-theme=dark] with @custom-variant, and an @theme inline block maps utilities to the CSS variables. There is no tailwind.config.js.\n157\t- No raw hex values in components. Components use semantic utilities only; a CI check greps for stray hex.\n158\t- A contrast test recomputes every text, UI and focus pair in both themes against WCAG 2.2 AA (4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text and UI) and fails the build on any miss.\n159\t- Spring and duration values that JavaScript needs are mirrored in CSS variables, with src/anim/tokens.ts as the canonical source for springs.\n160\t\n161\t## Animation layer\n162\t\n163\tsrc/anim/index.ts is the only animation import path for the rest of the app. It exposes a handful of semantic components and hooks, not library primitives.\n164\t\n165\t| Interaction class | Owner |\n166\t|-------------------|-------|\n167\t| Mount and unmount, presence, exit animations | motion |\n168\t| Layout and shared-element transitions | motion |\n169\t| Scroll-linked values and in-view reveals | motion |\n170\t| Text and number effects, stagger, ready-made motion-primitives prefabs | motion |\n171\t| Velocity-carrying physics: press-squish, rail hover lift with neighbor shift, theme toggle thumb | react-spring |\n172\t| Color and hover transitions, native scroll-snap rail swipe | CSS |\n173\t| Desktop wheel smoothing only | Lenis |\n174\t\n175\tThe rule: one library animates any given element. Never both libraries on one element or one property, and no Tailwind transition utilities on an element a library animates. If a vendored primitive duplicates a physics wrapper, one implementation is chosen per interaction and the other stays unused.\n176\t\n177\tSpring and duration tokens are defined once in src/anim/tokens.ts, exported in both libraries' formats (motion's stiffness and damping, react-spring's tension and friction), so the two feel like one system. Fine-pointer effects such as tilt, magnetic pull and spotlight are gated by (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine); touch gets a tap or press equivalent, or nothing. Every wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards className, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n178\t\n179\t## Reduced motion\n180\t\n181\tOne preference source, useMotionPreference, combines the OS prefers-reduced-motion setting with an optional site override and feeds three consumers:\n182\t\n183\t- motion: the provider renders MotionConfig with its reducedMotion setting. This disables transform and layout animation but not opacity or color, so wrappers handle parallax, scroll-linked and autoplay effects explicitly.\n184\t- react-spring: the provider calls Globals.assign({ skipAnimation }) so every spring jumps to its target; the effect cleans up on change.\n185\t- CSS: a @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) guard in base.css shortens transitions and animations and keeps scroll behavior native.\n186\t\n187\tContent is visible by default and is never hidden until JavaScript runs. Hidden initial states are applied client-side inside wrappers, and a noscript rule guarantees visibility. Pressed, focused and selected states must read without motion (color, outline, shape). Lenis is off under reduced motion. The provider listens for changes to the media query so a live OS toggle takes effect without a reload.\n188\t\n189\t## Assets\n190\t\n191\t- Images live in src/assets/images/ and are imported as modules (hashed, long-cached). vite-imagetools produces AVIF and WebP variants and srcset with intrinsic width and height to prevent layout shift. Below-the-fold images lazy-load; the hero image is high priority.\n192\t- Fonts are self-hosted through Fontsource (variable, latin subset, font-display swap), at most two families. No third-party font CDN.\n193\t- Brand icons (GitHub, LinkedIn, Instagram, email) are inline SVG, because the UI icon set has no brand glyphs. See the ADR log.\n194\t- The resume PDF served from public/ is a phone-number-free copy of the source resume.\n195\t- Only images that are actually used migrate from the previous site, with EXIF metadata stripped. Template leftovers are left behind.\n196\t\n197\t## Build and deploy\n198\t\n199\tGitHub Actions builds the site and publishes it to GitHub Pages. The Vite base comes from the VITE_BASE environment variable: the project URL path (for example /aa-phan-portfolio-v2/) while staging, and / after the cutover to the user-site URL. Assets are module-imported so no absolute asset paths exist; anything under public/ that JavaScript references goes through import.meta.env.BASE_URL. CI also builds with the project-path base so a broken sub-path is caught before launch. Steps, settings, cutover and rollback are in the deploy runbook (deploy-runbook.md).\n200\t\n201\t## Quality gates\n202\t\n203\t| Gate | Command | Fails the build when |\n204\t|------|---------|----------------------|\n205\t| Typecheck | `tsc -b` | A content field is missing or a reference is wrong |\n206\t| Lint with import boundaries | oxlint, warnings denied | A section imports an animation library or content data |\n207\t| Format check | Prettier with Tailwind class sorting | A file is not formatted |\n208\t| Unit tests | Vitest: shell parity, theme logic, motion preference, gate negatives | Any test fails |\n209\t| Path-case check | `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`, run locally and on Linux CI | An import differs in case from the file on disk |\n210\t| Raw color check | `scripts/check-colors.mjs` | Source outside the token file and vendored primitives holds a raw hex, rgb, hsl, oklch or oklab value, or a Tailwind default palette utility |\n211\t| Build | `vite build` | The Vite build errors |\n212\t| Initial JS budget | `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` | The module script and modulepreloads exceed 150 kB gzipped |\n213\t| Phone-number check | `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` over the built site | A served file holds a phone-shaped string or a tel: link |\n214\t| Token contrast test | Lands with the contrast gate | Any text, UI or focus pair is below AA in either theme |\n215\t| Smoke tests | Playwright in CI | The hero is missing with JavaScript off, the theme flashes, or content is hidden or a transform animation runs with reduced motion emulated |\n216\t| axe | Later gate | Serious or critical violations in light or dark |\n217\t| Mobile Lighthouse | Later gate | Targets set in the scaffold milestone are missed |\n218\t\n219\t`npm run verify` chains the first nine rows in that order, and both CI workflows call it.\n220\t\n221\t## Stack\n222\t\n223\tVerified 2026-10-08; {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PVJf4CMbzpfcp4sY6X3HWy","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// Phone-number gate over the built site.\n2\t//\n3\t// Everything in dist is served to the world, and CI logs of a public repository\n4\t// are public too. This fails when a served file holds a phone-shaped string or a\n5\t// tel: link. On a hit it prints only the file and byte offset, never the\n6\t// matched characters.\n7\t//\n8\t// Flag: --root DIR (default cwd); scans ROOT/dist.\n9\t// Exit: 0 printing \"no-phone: OK\", 1 on a hit, 2 when dist is missing.\n10\timport fs from 'node:fs'\n11\timport path from 'node:path'\n12\timport zlib from 'node:zlib'\n13\t\n14\tconst argv = process.argv.slice(2)\n15\tconst rootFlag = argv.indexOf('--root')\n16\tconst ROOT = path.resolve(rootFlag >= 0 && argv[rootFlag + 1] ? argv[rootFlag + 1] : process.cwd())\n17\tconst DIST = path.join(ROOT, 'dist')\n18\t\n19\tif (!fs.existsSync(DIST) || !fs.statSync(DIST).isDirectory()) {\n20\t  console.error(`no-phone: ${path.relative(ROOT, DIST) || DIST} not found, run the build first`)\n21\t  process.exit(2)\n22\t}\n23\t\n24\tconst SCANNED = /\\.(html|js|css|json|txt|svg|xml|webmanifest|pdf)$/i\n25\t// Markup and text files, where prose can name a number outright. A PDF's decoded text\n26\t// layer reads as prose too, so extracted PDF text is scanned as text (see TEXT_NAME).\n27\t// The PDF's own bytes are not: xref offsets are ten-digit runs.\n28\tconst MARKUP_FILES = /\\.(html|txt)$/i\n29\tconst TEXT_NAME = 'extracted.txt'\n30\tconst PDF_FILES = /\\.pdf$/i\n31\t\n32\t// A tel: link is a URI scheme, so it sits right after a quote, backtick, opening\n33\t// parenthesis, equals sign or tag end (or after whitespace in markup and text). A bare\n34\t// key such as {tel:true} in minified JS, which React DOM ships in its list of input\n35\t// types, is not a link and is not flagged.\n36\t//\n37\t// Separators between the digit groups, in any run: hyphen, dot, slash, space, tab, and the\n38\t// typographic dashes and spaces a word processor or resume produces (en and em dash,\n39\t// non-breaking hyphen, minus sign, no-break, narrow no-break, thin, figure and the other\n40\t// Unicode spaces). Each is matched as the character itself, as its UTF-8 bytes (files are\n41\t// read as latin1, one character per byte), as a JS \\uXXXX escape, and as an HTML entity.\n42\t// A leading +CC or 1 country prefix and a parenthesised area code are allowed.\n43\tconst SEPARATOR_CODE_POINTS = [\n44\t  0xa0,\n45\t  0xad,\n46\t  ...Array.from({ length: 0x0b }, (_, i) => 0x2000 + i), // U+2000 to U+200A spaces\n47\t  ...Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => 0x2010 + i), // U+2010 to U+2015 hyphens and dashes\n48\t  0x202f,\n49\t  0x205f,\n50\t  0x2212,\n51\t  0x3000,\n52\t]\n53\tconst hex = (cp, width) => cp.toString(16).padStart(width, '0')\n54\tconst SEPARATOR_FORMS = [\n55\t  '[-. \\\\t/\\\\x96\\\\x97]', // plain ASCII, plus WinAnsi en and em dash in extracted PDF text\n56\t  `[${SEPARATOR_CODE_POINTS.map((cp) => `\\\\u${hex(cp, 4)}`).join('')}]`,\n57\t  ...SEPARATOR_CODE_POINTS.map((cp) =>\n58\t    [...Buffer.from(String.fromCodePoint(cp), 'utf8')].map((b) => `\\\\x${hex(b, 2)}`).join(''),\n59\t  ),\n60\t  ...SEPARATOR_CODE_POINTS.map((cp) => `\\\\\\\\u${hex(cp, 4)}`),\n61\t  ...SEPARATOR_CODE_POINTS.map((cp) => `&#(?:${cp}|x0*${hex(cp, 1)});`),\n62\t  '&(?:nbsp|ensp|emsp|thinsp|numsp|hyphen|dash|ndash|mdash|horbar|minus);',\n63\t]\n64\tconst SEP = `(?:${SEPARATOR_FORMS.join('|')})`\n65\tconst PREFIX = `(?:\\\\+\\\\d{1,2}${SEP}*|1${SEP}+)?`\n66\t\n67\t// A plausible North American number: area code and exchange start 2 to 9, with an optional\n68\t// 1 or +1 country prefix. Used for runs with no separators at all, where a loose ten-digit\n69\t// pattern would also match hashes and numeric constants. The 32-bit integer limits (and\n70\t// the few values below the signed one) are excluded, since a quoted 2147483647 is a CSS\n71\t// z-index in many libraries.\n72\tconst NANP_RUN =\n73\t  '(?<![0-9A-Za-z])(?!214748364\\\\d|429496729[56])(?:\\\\+?1)?[2-9]\\\\d{2}[2-9]\\\\d{6}(?![0-9A-Za-z])'\n74\t\n75\t// Where a shape applies. `raw` is the file as read (for a PDF, with stream bodies masked\n76\t// out), `literal` is the string literals of a JS or JSON file, `stream` is a decoded PDF\n77\t// content stream and `text` is the text a PDF content stream draws.\n78\tconst LITERAL_FILES = /\\.(js|mjs|cjs|json|webmanifest)$/i\n79\tconst SHAPES = [\n80\t  {\n81\t    label: 'separated number',\n82\t    views: ['raw', 'text'],\n83\t    re: new RegExp(\n84\t      `(?<!\\\\d)${PREFIX}(?:\\\\(\\\\d{3}\\\\)${SEP}*|\\\\d{3}${SEP}+)\\\\d{3}${SEP}+\\\\d{4}(?!\\\\d)`,\n85\t      'gi',\n86\t    ),\n87\t  },\n88\t  { label: 'tel: link', views: ['raw', 'text', 'stream'], re: /(?<=[\"'`(=>]|^)tel:/gim },\n89\t  { label: 'tel: link', views: ['raw', 'text'], re: /(?<=\\s)tel:/gi, only: MARKUP_FILES },\n90\t  // Standalone digit runs are checked in markup and text only: minified JS legitimately\n91\t  // holds ten-digit constants. A number in a JS string literal is caught by the `literal`\n92\t  // view, which ignores code outside strings.\n93\t  {\n94\t    label: 'ten-digit run',\n95\t    views: ['raw', 'text'],\n96\t    re: /(?<![0-9A-Za-z])\\d{10}(?![0-9A-Za-z])/g,\n97\t    only: MARKUP_FILES,\n98\t  },\n99\t  {\n100\t    label: 'eleven-digit run',\n101\t    views: ['raw', 'text'],\n102\t    re: /(?<![0-9A-Za-z])\\+?1[2-9]\\d{2}[2-9]\\d{6}(?![0-9A-Za-z])/g,\n103\t    only: MARKUP_FILES,\n104\t  },\n105\t  { label: 'unseparated number', views: ['literal'], re: new RegExp(NANP_RUN, 'g') },\n106\t]\n107\t\n108\t// The string literals of a JS or JSON file, kept at their byte offsets with everything else\n109\t// blanked to NUL, so a numeric constant, an identifier or a comment can never match. This is\n110\t// a small tokenizer, not a parser: comments and regex literals are skipped so an apostrophe in\n111\t// either does not shift which quotes open a string, and the `${...}` expressions inside a\n112\t// template literal count as code (minified bundles hold numeric constants there).\n113\tconst REGEX_AFTER = new Set([...'(,=:[!&|?{};+-*%<>~^'])\n114\tfunction literalView(text) {\n115\t  const n = text.length\n116\t  const view = Buffer.alloc(n)\n117\t  const keep = (from, to) => view.write(text.slice(from, to), from, 'latin1')\n118\t  const templateDepth = [] // one entry per open `${`: the `{` nesting inside it\n119\t  let i = 0\n120\t  let prev = '' // last non-space character of code, for telling a regex from a division\n121\t\n122\t  // i is just past an opening backtick or a closing brace of a `${...}`.\n123\t  const templateBody = () => {\n124\t    const from = i\n125\t    while (i < n) {\n126\t      if (text[i] === '\\\\') i += 2\n127\t      else if (text[i] === '`') {\n128\t        keep(from, i)\n129\t        i++\n130\t        return\n131\t      } else if (text[i] === '$' && text[i + 1] === '{') {\n132\t        keep(from, i)\n133\t        i += 2\n134\t        templateDepth.push(0)\n135\t        return\n136\t      } else i++\n137\t    }\n138\t    keep(from, n)\n139\t  }\n140\t\n141\t  while (i < n) {\n142\t    const c = text[i]\n143\t    const next = text[i + 1]\n144\t    if (c === '/' && next === '*') {\n145\t      const end = text.indexOf('*/', i + 2)\n146\t      i = end < 0 ? n : end + 2\n147\t    } else if (c === '/' && next === '/') {\n148\t      const end = text.indexOf('\\n', i)\n149\t      i = end < 0 ? n : end\n150\t    } else if (c === '\"' || c === \"'\") {\n151\t      const from = ++i\n152\t      while (i < n && text[i] !== c && text[i] !== '\\n') i += text[i] === '\\\\' ? 2 : 1\n153\t      keep(from, i)\n154\t      i++\n155\t      prev = c\n156\t    } else if (c === '`') {\n157\t      i++\n158\t      templateBody()\n159\t      prev = c\n160\t    } else if (\n161\t      c === '/' &&\n162\t      (prev === '' ||\n163\t        REGEX_AFTER.has(prev) ||\n164\t        /\\b(?:return|typeof)\\s*$/.test(text.slice(i - 10, i)))\n165\t    ) {\n166\t      let inClass = false\n167\t      for (i++; i < n && text[i] !== '\\n'; i++) {\n168\t        if (text[i] === '\\\\') i++\n169\t        else if (text[i] === '[') inClass = true\n170\t        else if (text[i] === ']') inClass = false\n171\t        else if (text[i] === '/' && !inClass) break\n172\t      }\n173\t      i++\n174\t      prev = '/'\n175\t    } else {\n176\t      if (templateDepth.length > 0) {\n177\t        if (c === '{') templateDepth[templateDepth.length - 1]++\n178\t        else if (c === '}') {\n179\t          if (templateDepth[templateDepth.length - 1] === 0) {\n180\t            templateDepth.pop()\n181\t            i++\n182\t            templateBody()\n183\t            continue\n184\t          }\n185\t          templateDepth[templateDepth.length - 1]--\n186\t        }\n187\t      }\n188\t      if (c > ' ') prev = c\n189\t      i++\n190\t    }\n191\t  }\n192\t  return view.toString('latin1')\n193\t}\n194\t\n195\t// PDF text lives in content streams, usually Flate-compressed, so a raw byte scan of the\n196\t// file cannot see it. Each stream is inflated when it inflates (otherwise its raw bytes are\n197\t// taken as an uncompressed content stream), then the strings it draws (Tj and TJ operators)\n198\t// are pulled out so a number split across kerned runs still reads as one. The number shapes\n199\t// apply to that drawn text only. Stream bytes are never scanned for them: drawing operands\n200\t// are decimals and integers (a move to a three-decimal x and a four-digit y) that read as a\n201\t// separated number, and xref offsets are ten-digit runs. Stream bodies are blanked out of the raw scan for the same\n202\t// reason, so the raw scan still covers the PDF's own dictionaries (document info strings,\n203\t// URI actions) at their byte offsets. tel: links are checked in decoded streams too.\n204\t// Limit: text set in a font with a custom or CID encoding stores glyph ids, not characters,\n205\t// and is not readable without the font's ToUnicode map, and strings inside compressed object\n206\t// streams are not read as text. The resume PDF is also reviewed by hand for that reason.\n207\tfunction pdfStreams(buf) {\n208\t  const text = buf.toString('latin1')\n209\t  const streams = []\n210\t  const open = /stream\\r?\\n/g\n211\t  let m\n212\t  while ((m = open.exec(text)) !== null) {\n213\t    const start = m.index + m[0].length\n214\t    const end = text.indexOf('endstream', start)\n215\t    if (end < 0) break\n216\t    // Resume after \"endstream\", whose tail would otherwise match \"stream\" again.\n217\t    open.lastIndex = end + 'endstream'.length\n218\t    const slice = buf.subarray(start, end)\n219\t    let content = slice\n220\t    try {\n221\t      content = zlib.inflateSync(slice, { finishFlush: zlib.constants.Z_SYNC_FLUSH })\n222\t    } catch {\n223\t      // Not Flate (an image, a '\\n', r: '\\r', t: '\\t', b: '\\b', f: '\\f' }[ch] ?? ch\n246\t  })\n247\t}\n248\t\n249\tfunction decodePdfHex(body) {\n250\t  const hex = body.replace(/\\s+/g, '')\n251\t  const padded = hex.length % 2 ? `${hex}0` : hex\n252\t  const bytes = Buffer.from(padded, 'hex')\n253\t  // A byte order mark marks UTF-16BE text, whose dashes and spaces are real characters.\n254\t  if (bytes[0] === 0xfe && bytes[1] === 0xff) return bytes.subarray(2).swap16().toString('utf16le')\n255\t  // Otherwise dropping NULs reads two-byte text whose characters are plain ASCII.\n256\t  return bytes.toString('latin1').replaceAll('\\0', '')\n257\t}\n258\t\n259\tfunction pdfShownText(content) {\n260\t  const out = []\n261\t  const operand = String.raw`\\((?:\\\\[\\s\\S]|[^()\\\\])*\\)|<[0-9A-Fa-f\\s]*>`\n262\t  const tj = new RegExp(`\\\\[((?:${operand}|[^\\\\]()<])*)\\\\]\\\\s*TJ|(${operand})\\\\s*(?:Tj|'|\")`, 'g')\n263\t  const item = for (const m of content.matchAll(tj)) {\n267\t    if (m[2] !== undefined) {\n268\t      out.push(one(m[2]))\n269\t      continue\n270\t    }\n271\t    let line = ''\n272\t    for (const part of += ' '\n276\t    }\n277\t    out.push(line)\n278\t  }\n279\t  return out.join(' ')\n280\t}\n281\t\n282\tfunction walk(dir, out) {\n283\t  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {\n284\t    const full = path.join(dir, entry.name)\n285\t    if (entry.isDirectory()) walk(full, out)\n286\t    else if (entry.isFile() && SCANNED.test(entry.name)) out.push(full)\n287\t  }\n288\t}\n289\t\n290\tconst files = []\n291\twalk(DIST, files)\n292\t\n293\tconst hits = []\n294\t// `name` is matched against a shape's `only` filter; `view` selects the shapes that read refreshed: 2026-11-20 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-20\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 Browser (static bundle)                      │\n│                                                              │\n│  index.html  (inline theme-init script, noscript-safe shell) │\n│        │                                                     │\n│        ▼                                                     │\n│  src/main.tsx  ──►  src/app/App.tsx                          │\n│                        │                                     │\n│            ┌───────────┴────────────┐                        │\n│            ▼                        ▼                        │\n│  src/app/Providers.tsx     src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx │\n│  (MotionPreferenceProvider)       │                          │\n│            │                      ▼                          │\n│            ▼               src/lib/theme.ts + useTheme.ts    │\n│  src/app/Page.tsx                 │  (html[data-theme])      │\n│     ├── src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx                           │\n│     │      └── src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx           │\n│     └── src/sections/Stubs/SectionStubs.tsx                  │\n│            └── src/layout/Section.tsx + Container.tsx        │\n│                                                              │\n│  src/anim/  (only layer importing motion/react and           │\n│              @react-spring/web)                              │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n          │\n          ▼\n  public/resume.pdf, public/favicon.svg, @fontsource Inter (static)\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mount React root in StrictMode, load font CSS and global styles | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App shell | Compose providers, fixed theme toggle header, page | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Wrap the tree in the motion preference provider | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Assemble `<main id=\"main\">` from sections | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Hero | Name, tagline, resume download, mailto and `#contact` links | `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` |\n| Section stubs | Six placeholder sections (Experience, Projects, About, Skills, Education, Contact) | `src/sections/Stubs/SectionStubs.tsx` |\n| Section / Container | Shared section and max-width layout wrappers | `src/layout/Section.tsx`, `src/layout/Container.tsx` |\n| ThemeToggle | Accessible switch button; writes the theme on click | `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` |\n| Theme logic | Read/write `html[data-theme]`, `localStorage`, system and cross-tab sync | `src/lib/theme.ts` |\n| useTheme | `useSyncExternalStore` binding of the theme | `src/lib/useTheme.ts` |\n| Motion preference | Single reduced-motion source; feeds MotionConfig and react-spring | `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` |\n| Animation tokens | Spring and duration values shared by motion and react-spring | `src/anim/tokens.ts` |\n| TextReveal | Word-by-word reveal with lazy-loaded motion chunk and static fallbacks | `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx` |\n| TextEffect (vendored) | motion-primitives text animation, copied into the repo | `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` |\n| ThemeThumb (physics) | react-spring-driven toggle thumb | `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` |\n| Styles | Tailwind v4 entry, token mirror, base rules and reduced-motion guard | `src/styles/index.css`, `src/styles/tokens.css`, `src/styles/base.css` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page, client-only React application with a flat composition tree and no router, no global store, and no data fetching.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One route: the whole site is one `<main>` on one document. Sections are anchors, not pages.\n- No content data layer yet: copy is inline JSX in `src/sections/**`. `src/content/`, `src/hooks/`, and `src/assets/` described in `docs/architecture.md` do not exist in the tree yet.\n- Three React contexts or external stores at most: the motion preference (via `MotionConfig` in `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`) and the theme (via `useSyncExternalStore` in `src/lib/useTheme.ts`, backed by the DOM attribute rather than React state).\n- The DOM is the source of truth for theme: `html[data-theme]` is written by the inline script in `index.html`, by `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`, and read by `readTheme`.\n- Static output: `vite build` produces `dist/`, deployed to GitHub Pages.\n\n## Layers\n\n**Bootstrap / app shell (`src/main.tsx`, `src/app/`):**\n- Purpose: mount the root, compose providers, assemble the page.\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`, `src/app/Page.tsx`\n- Contains: component composition only; no logic beyond provider wiring.\n- Depends on: `src/anim` (provider), `src/components/ui`, `src/sections`\n- Used by: Vite entry (`index.html` loads `/src/main.tsx`)\n\n**Sections (`src/sections/`):**\n- Purpose: page content blocks.\n- Location: `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/sections/Stubs/SectionStubs.tsx`\n- Contains: JSX with inline copy.\n- Depends on: `src/layout`, `src/anim` (barrel and `TextReveal`), `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout (`src/layout/`):**\n- Purpose: consistent section spacing and width.\n- Location: `src/layout/Section.tsx`, `src/layout/Container.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Used by: sections\n\n**UI components (`src/components/ui/`):**\n- Purpose: reusable interactive controls.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` (only component present; `Button`, `Tag`, `Card`, `Heading`, `Logo`, `Icon` are planned, not built)\n- Depends on: `src/anim` (barrel, `ThemeThumb`), `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/lib/useTheme.ts`\n- Used by: `src/app/App.tsx`\n\n**Vendored primitives (`src/components/motion-primitives/`):**\n- Purpose: owned copy of third-party animation source.\n- Location: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Used by: `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx` via `React.lazy`\n\n**Animation layer (`src/anim/`):**\n- Purpose: the only place that imports `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web`, and the only path for the rest of the app (through `src/anim/index.ts`).\n- Location: `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`, `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`, `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/components/motion-primitives/`, `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Used by: sections, UI components, app providers\n\n**Lib (`src/lib/`):**\n- Purpose: framework-free helpers and the theme store.\n- Location: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/lib/useTheme.ts`, `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Depends on: DOM APIs only (`localStorage`, `matchMedia`, `MutationObserver`)\n- Used by: UI components, layout, anim\n\n**Styles (`src/styles/`):**\n- Purpose: Tailwind v4 entry, design tokens, base rules.\n- Location: `src/styles/index.css` (imports Tailwind, `@custom-variant dark`, `@theme inline` mapping), `src/styles/tokens.css` (role and derived color tokens per theme), `src/styles/base.css` (resets, focus, reduced-motion guard, `.reveal-pending`)\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` imports `./styles/index.css`\n\n**Static document (`index.html`, `public/`):**\n- Purpose: HTML shell with pre-rendered hero and section headings, theme-init script, static assets.\n- Location: `index.html`, `public/favicon.svg`, `public/resume.pdf`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (first load)\n\n1. Browser requests `index.html`; the inline script sets `html[data-theme]` from `localStorage` key `theme`, falling back to `prefers-color-scheme` (`index.html`, lines 9-24).\n2. The static shell (hero text, section headings) is visible before JS runs (`index.html` `<div id=\"root\">`).\n3. Module script `/src/main.tsx` loads, imports the Inter font CSS and `src/styles/index.css`, then renders `<App />` into `#root` (`src/main.tsx`).\n4. `App` renders `Providers` → `MotionPreferenceProvider`, which reads `useMotionPreference` and sets `MotionConfig` and `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })` (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`).\n5. `Page` renders `Hero` and `SectionStubs` (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n6. `TextReveal` renders a static paragraph immediately, then lazily loads the `TextEffect` chunk unless reduced motion is on (`src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`).\n\n### Theme Toggle Path\n\n1. User clicks the button in `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`; `toggle()` calls `applyTheme(next)`.\n2. `applyTheme` sets `html[data-theme]` first, then writes `localStorage` (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n3. `useTheme` (`useSyncExternalStore`) observes the attribute via `subscribeTheme`'s `MutationObserver` and re-renders the toggle with the new `aria-pressed` and thumb state.\n4. Cross-tab: a `storage` event triggers `syncAttribute(storedTheme() ?? systemTheme())`. OS scheme changes apply only when nothing is stored.\n\n### Reduced-Motion Path\n\n1. `useMotionPreference` subscribes to `(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` via `useSyncExternalStore` (`src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`).\n2. `MotionPreferenceProvider` passes `reducedMotion: 'always' | 'never'` to motion's `MotionConfig` and calls react-spring `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })`.\n3. `TextReveal` renders the plain paragraph and skips the lazy import when reduced.\n4. `src/styles/base.css` carries a CSS `prefers-reduced-motion` guard as the native fallback.\n\n**State Management:**\n- Theme: DOM attribute `html[data-theme]` plus `localStorage`. No React state owns it.\n- Motion preference: `useSyncExternalStore` over `matchMedia`.\n- No other global state. No forms, no fetching.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Animation barrel (`src/anim/index.ts`):**\n- Purpose: single public import path for animation primitives and tokens.\n- Exports: `ThemeThumb`, `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `useMotionPreference`, `TextReveal`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`.\n- Pattern: sections import from `@/anim`, never from `motion/react` directly.\n\n**Theme store (`src/lib/theme.ts`):**\n- Purpose: the one place that reads and writes the theme.\n- Exports: `Theme`, `THEME_STORAGE_KEY`, `readTheme`, `applyTheme`, `subscribeTheme`.\n- Pattern: the HTML attribute is the single source of truth; storage and React are derived from it.\n\n**Motion tokens (`src/anim/tokens.ts`):**\n- Purpose: one table of spring values in both libraries' formats (motion `stiffness`/`damping`, react-spring `tension`/`friction`).\n- Pattern: `springs.snappy | gentle | bouncy`, `durations.fast | base | slow`, `easeOut`.\n\n**Section primitive (`src/layout/Section.tsx`):**\n- Purpose: `<section id aria-labelledby>` with `<h2>` heading and `Container`.\n- Pattern: props in (`id`, `title`, `className`, `children`), JSX out.\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser document:**\n- Location: `index.html`\n- Triggers: any navigation to the site root or `base` path.\n- Responsibilities: pre-theme, static shell, module script tag.\n\n**Module entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: `<script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\">` in `index.html`.\n- Responsibilities: `createRoot(#root).render(<StrictMode><App/></StrictMode>)`.\n\n**Build entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `npm run dev`, `npm run build`, `npm run preview`.\n- Responsibilities: base path, plugins, `@` alias.\n\n**Test entries:**\n- Unit: `vitest.config.ts` (`tests/unit/**`), setup `tests/setup.ts`.\n- E2E: `playwright.config.ts` (`tests/e2e/**`), local preview on port 4173 or `E2E_BASE_URL`.\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No workers. No server.\n- **Global state:** `document.documentElement` `data-theme` attribute and `localStorage` key `theme` are shared mutable state (`src/lib/theme.ts`). `Globals` from `@react-spring/web` is module-level and is assigned in `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`.\n- **Circular imports:** None observed. The `@/anim` barrel re-exports `ThemeThumb`, which imports from `@/anim/...` files; keep the barrel free of cycles when adding exports.\n- **Lazy boundary:** `motion/react` text effects are split into their own chunk by `React.lazy` in `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`. Keep the static path free of motion imports to protect the initial JS budget (150 kB gzipped, `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`).\n- **Planned import rules:** `docs/architecture.md` specifies that only `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/` may import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web`, and that only `src/app/Page.tsx` imports content data. These rules are documented as enforced by `.oxlintrc.json` `no-restricted-imports`. Check `.oxlintrc.json` before relying on them.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing animation libraries from sections or UI\n\n**What happens:** A component imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** It bypasses the motion-preference wiring and the token table, and breaks the layer rule in `docs/architecture.md`.\n**Do this instead:** Add a semantic wrapper in `src/anim/` and export it from `src/anim/index.ts`, as `TextReveal` and `ThemeThumb` do.\n\n### Animating the same element with both libraries\n\n**What happens:** A single element receives both a motion animation and a react-spring value.\n**Why it's wrong:** Both write `transform`; the outcomes fight and the reduced-motion handling becomes unclear.\n**Do this instead:** Pick one owner per interaction, following the table in `docs/architecture.md` (motion for mount/exit/layout/scroll, react-spring for velocity physics).\n\n### Writing the theme attribute directly\n\n**What happens:** Code calls `document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', ...)` or writes `localStorage` outside `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Bypasses the storage-blocked fallback and cross-tab sync logic in `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n**Do this instead:** Call `applyTheme(theme)` from `src/lib/theme.ts`. Read with `useTheme()` from `src/lib/useTheme.ts`.\n\n### Hardcoded asset paths\n\n**What happens:** Links to `/resume.pdf` or `/favicon.svg` are written without the base.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks when `VITE_BASE` changes from `/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/` to `/`.\n**Do this instead:** Use `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` as `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` does, or `%BASE_URL%` in `index.html`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Degrade to static content. Never leave the page blank.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `ChunkBoundary` class component in `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx` catches a failed lazy chunk load and renders the plain paragraph.\n- `Suspense` fallback in `TextReveal` renders the same paragraph while the chunk loads.\n- Storage access (`localStorage`) and `matchMedia` are wrapped in try/catch in `src/lib/theme.ts` and `index.html`.\n- No global error boundary. Referenced through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`.\n- Contains: `favicon.svg`, `resume.pdf`.\n\n**`scripts/`:**\n- Purpose: build-time gates run by `npm run verify`.\n- Contains: `check-colors.mjs` (no raw color literals outside tokens), `check-contrast.mjs` (WCAG pairs from `contrast-pairs.json`), `check-js-budget.mjs` (150 kB gz initial JS), `check-no-phone.mjs` (scans `dist/`), `check-path-case.mjs` (import case vs disk), `lib/color.mjs`, `lib/tokens.mjs`.\n\n**`tests/`:**\n- Purpose: test code, separate from `src/`.\n- Contains: `unit/` (Vitest: `boundaries.test.ts`, `gates.test.ts`, `hero-parity.test.tsx`, `layout.test.tsx`, `motion-preference.test.tsx`, `theme-script.test.ts`, `tokens.test.ts`); `e2e/` (Playwright: `smoke.spec.ts`, `motion.spec.ts`); `fixtures/` (sample files used by boundary and gate tests, for example `fixtures/anim/Imports.tsx`); `setup.ts` (Vitest setup).\n\n**`docs/`:**\n- Purpose: authoritative design and decision docs.\n- Contains: `architecture.md`, `deploy-runbook.md`, `contrast-matrix.md`, `adr/0001..0008` plus `adr/README.md`.\n\n**`.planning/`:**\n- Purpose: GSD planning. Not shipped.\n- Contains: `PROJECT.md`, `REQUIREMENTS.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, `STATE.md`, `WINDOWS.md`, `phases/01-…` to `03-…` (PLAN, SUMMARY, REVIEW, VERIFICATION, and similar), `codebase/` (these map documents).\n\n**`.github/workflows/`:**\n- Purpose: CI and deployment.\n- Contains: `ci.yml`, `deploy.yml`. Actions are pinned to commit SHAs.\n\n## Key File Locations\n\n**Entry Points:**\n- `index.html`: HTML document and inline theme-init script.\n- `src/main.tsx`: React root and global CSS/font imports.\n- `src/app/App.tsx`: app shell.\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: build and dev config, `@` alias, `VITE_BASE`.\n- `tsconfig.app.json`: strict app TypeScript config with `@/*` paths.\n- `.oxlintrc.json`: lint rules including import boundaries.\n- `prettier.config.mjs`: formatting.\n- `vitest.config.ts`, `playwright.config.ts`: test runners.\n- `package.json`: scripts and dependency versions.\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: theme state.\n- `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`: reduced-motion source.\n- `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`: text reveal with fallbacks.\n- `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`: react-spring toggle thumb.\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`: Vitest suites.\n- `tests/e2e/`: Playwright suites.\n- `tests/fixtures/`: fixtures for gate tests.\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`, one exported component per file, for example `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`, `src/layout/Section.tsx`.\n- Hooks: `use` prefix camelCase `.ts`, for example `src/lib/useTheme.ts`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`.\n- Non-component modules: camelCase `.ts`, for example `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`, `src/lib/utils.ts`.\n- Exception: vendored `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` uses kebab-case, matching upstream.\n- Section folders: PascalCase, for example `src/sections/Hero/`, with a file of the same name inside (`Hero.tsx`).\n- Stub folder: `src/sections/Stubs/`.\n- Tests: `*.test.ts` or `*.test.tsx` under `tests/unit/`; `*.spec.ts` under `tests/e2e/`.\n- Scripts: `check-<thing>.mjs` under `scripts/`.\n- Docs: UPPERCASE for planning files, numbered ADRs `docs/adr/NNNN-kebab-title.md`.\n\n**Directories:**\n- Lowercase for feature and layer folders: `src/app`, `src/anim`, `src/lib`, `src/layout`, `src/styles`.\n- PascalCase for section folders under `src/sections/`.\n- Sub-categories inside `anim/`: `declarative/` (motion-owned), `physics/` (react-spring-owned).\n\n**Exports:**\n- Named exports throughout (`export function Hero()`, `export function Section()`). The app uses no default exports for components. Exception: `text-effect` lazy import maps `m.TextEffect` to `default` in `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`.\n- Animation public API goes through `src/anim/index.ts`.\n\n**Path alias:**\n- `@/` maps to `src/` (`@/anim`, `@/lib/utils`, `@/sections/Hero/Hero`). Defined in `vite.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`, and `tsconfig.app.json`.\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New section (for example About):**\n- Implementation: `src/sections/About/About.tsx`; replace the matching `<Section>` stub in `src/sections/Stubs/SectionStubs.tsx` by moving it into `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n- Use `Section` from `src/layout/Section.tsx` for the wrapper and heading.\n- Tests: `tests/unit/` for logic, `tests/e2e/` for user flows.\n\n**New animated component:**\n- Wrapper: `src/anim/declarative/<Name>.tsx` (motion) or `src/anim/physics/<Name>.tsx` (react-spring).\n- Export from `src/anim/index.ts`. Read `useMotionPreference` and tokens from `src/anim/tokens.ts`.\n- Do not import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` from any folder other than `src/anim/` or `src/components/motion-primitives/`.\n\n**New reusable UI control:**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`.\n- Use semantic color utilities (`bg-primary`, `text-muted`), never raw hex (see `scripts/check-colors.mjs`).\n\n**New shared helper:**\n- Framework-free: `src/lib/<name>.ts`.\n- Class merging: use `cn` from `src/lib/utils.ts`.\n\n**New design token:**\n- Role or derived color: `src/styles/tokens.css`, with the mapping in `src/styles/index.css` `@theme inline`.\n- Animation spring or duration: `src/anim/tokens.ts` (JS), mirror in `src/styles/tokens.css` (`--dur-*`, `--ease-out`).\n\n**New static asset:**\n- URL-stable file: `public/`, linked with `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`.\n- Imported asset: `src/` and import as a module, so Vite hashes it.\n\n**New test:**\n- Unit: `tests/unit/<name>.test.ts(x)`. Vitest resolves `@/` aliases.\n- E2E: `tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts`.
