Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mount React root in StrictMode, load font CSS and global styles | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App shell | Compose providers, fixed theme toggle header, page | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Wrap the tree in the motion preference provider | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Assemble `<main id=\"main\">` from sections | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Hero | Name, tagline, resume download, mailto and `#contact` links | `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` |\n| Section stubs | Six placeholder sections (Experience, Projects, About, Skills, Education, Contact) | `src/sections/Stubs/SectionStubs.tsx` |\n| Section / Container | Shared section and max-width layout wrappers | `src/layout/Section.tsx`, `src/layout/Container.tsx` |\n| ThemeToggle | Accessible switch button; writes the theme on click | `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` |\n| Theme logic | Read/write `html[data-theme]`, `localStorage`, system and cross-tab sync | `src/lib/theme.ts` |\n| useTheme | `useSyncExternalStore` binding of the theme | `src/lib/useTheme.ts` |\n| Motion preference | Single reduced-motion source; feeds MotionConfig and react-spring | `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx` |\n| Animation tokens | Spring and duration values shared by motion and react-spring | `src/anim/tokens.ts` |\n| TextReveal | Word-by-word reveal with lazy-loaded motion chunk and static fallbacks | `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx` |\n| TextEffect (vendored) | motion-primitives text animation, copied into the repo | `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` |\n| ThemeThumb (physics) | react-spring-driven toggle thumb | `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` |\n| Styles | Tailwind v4 entry, token mirror, base rules and reduced-motion guard | `src/styles/index.css`, `src/styles/tokens.css`, `src/styles/base.css` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page, client-only React application with a flat composition tree. No router, no global store, no data fetching.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One route: the whole site is one `<main>` on one document. Sections are in-page anchors, not separate pages.\n- No content data layer yet: copy is inline JSX in `src/sections/**`. The `src/content/`, `src/hooks/` and `src/assets/` folders described in `docs/architecture.md` do not exist in the tree yet.\n- Shared state is minimal: the motion preference is provided through `MotionConfig` in `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, and the theme is read through `useSyncExternalStore` in `src/lib/useTheme.ts`, backed by the DOM attribute rather than React state.\n- The DOM is the source of truth for theme: `html[data-theme]` is written by the inline script in `index.html`, by `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`, and read by `readTheme`.\n- Static output: `vite build` produces `dist/`, deployed to GitHub Pages.\n\n## Layers\n\n**Bootstrap / app shell (`src/main.tsx`, `src/app/`):**\n- Purpose: mount the root, compose providers, assemble the page.\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`, `src/app/Page.tsx`\n- Contains: component composition and provider wiring only.\n- Depends on: `src/anim` (provider), `src/components/ui`, `src/sections`\n- Used by: Vite entry (`index.html` loads `/src/main.tsx`)\n\n**Sections (`src/sections/`):**\n- Purpose: page content blocks.\n- Location: `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`, `src/sections/Stubs/SectionStubs.tsx`\n- Contains: JSX with inline copy.\n- Depends on: `src/layout`, `src/anim` (barrel and `TextReveal`), `import.meta.env.BASE_URL`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`\n\n**Layout (`src/layout/`):**\n- Purpose: consistent section spacing and width.\n- Location: `src/layout/Section.tsx`, `src/layout/Container.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Used by: sections\n\n**UI components (`src/components/ui/`):**\n- Purpose: reusable interactive controls.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` (the only component present; `Button`, `Tag`, `Card`, `Heading`, `Logo` and `Icon` are planned, not built)\n- Depends on: `src/anim` (barrel, `ThemeThumb`), `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/lib/useTheme.ts`\n- Used by: `src/app/App.tsx`\n\n**Vendored primitives (`src/components/motion-primitives/`):**\n- Purpose: owned copy of third-party animation source.\n- Location: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Used by: `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx` via `React.lazy`\n\n**Animation layer (`src/anim/`):**\n- Purpose: the only place that imports `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web` (apart from the vendored primitives), and the public path for the rest of the app through `src/anim/index.ts`.\n- Location: `src/anim/index.ts`, `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`, `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`, `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`\n- Depends on: `src/components/motion-primitives/`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`\n- Used by: sections, UI components, app providers\n\n**Lib (`src/lib/`):**\n- Purpose: framework-free helpers and the theme store.\n- Location: `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/lib/useTheme.ts` (a React hook, so not framework-free), `src/lib/utils.ts`\n- Depends on: DOM APIs only (`localStorage`, `matchMedia`, `MutationObserver`)\n- Used by: UI components, layout, anim\n\n**Styles (`src/styles/`):**\n- Purpose: Tailwind v4 entry, design tokens, base rules.\n- Location: `src/styles/index.css` (imports Tailwind, `@custom-variant dark`, `@theme inline` mapping), `src/styles/tokens.css` (role and derived color tokens per theme), `src/styles/base.css` (resets, `.reveal-pending`, reduced-motion guard)\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` imports `./styles/index.css`\n\n**Static document (`index.html`, `public/`):**\n- Purpose: HTML shell with the static hero and section headings, theme-init script, static assets.\n- Location: `index.html`, `public/favicon.svg`, `public/resume.pdf`\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (first load)\n\n1. Browser requests `index.html`. The inline script sets `html[data-theme]` from `localStorage` key `theme`, falling back to `prefers-color-scheme` (`index.html`, the script in `<head>`).\n2. The static shell (hero text, section headings) is in the HTML before JS runs (`<div id=\"root\">` in `index.html`).\n3. Module script `/src/main.tsx` loads, imports the Inter font CSS and `src/styles/index.css`, then renders `<App />` into `#root` (`src/main.tsx`).\n4. `App` renders `Providers`, which wraps `MotionPreferenceProvider`. That reads `useMotionPreference`, sets `MotionConfig` and calls `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })` (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`).\n5. `Page` renders `Hero` and `SectionStubs` (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n6. `TextReveal` renders a static paragraph immediately, then lazily loads the `TextEffect` chunk unless reduced motion is on (`src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`).\n\n### Theme Toggle Path\n\n1. User clicks the button in `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx`. `toggle()` calls `applyTheme(next)`.\n2. `applyTheme` sets `html[data-theme]` first, then writes `localStorage` (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n3. `useTheme` (`useSyncExternalStore`) observes the attribute through the `MutationObserver` in `subscribeTheme` and re-renders the toggle with the new `aria-pressed` and thumb state.\n4. Cross-tab: a `storage` event triggers `syncAttribute(storedTheme() ?? systemTheme())`. OS scheme changes apply only while nothing is stored.\n\n### Reduced-Motion Path\n\n1. `useMotionPreference` subscribes to `(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` via `useSyncExternalStore` (`src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`).\n2. `MotionPreferenceProvider` passes `reducedMotion: 'always' | 'never'` to motion's `MotionConfig` and calls react-spring `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })`.\n3. `TextReveal` renders the plain paragraph and skips the lazy import when reduced.\n4. `src/styles/base.css` carries a CSS `prefers-reduced-motion` guard as the native fallback.\n\n**State Management:**\n- Theme: DOM attribute `html[data-theme]` plus `localStorage`. No React state owns it.\n- Motion preference: `useSyncExternalStore` over `matchMedia`.\n- No other global state. No forms, no fetching.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Animation barrel (`src/anim/index.ts`):**\n- Purpose: single public import path for animation primitives and tokens.\n- Exports: `ThemeThumb`, `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `useMotionPreference`, `TextReveal`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`.\n- Pattern: sections and UI import from `@/anim`, never from `motion/react` directly.\n\n**Theme store (`src/lib/theme.ts`):**\n- Purpose: the one place that reads and writes the theme.\n- Exports: `Theme`, `THEME_STORAGE_KEY`, `readTheme`, `applyTheme`, `subscribeTheme`.\n- Pattern: the HTML attribute is the single source of truth; storage and React state are derived from it.\n\n**Motion tokens (`src/anim/tokens.ts`):**\n- Purpose: one table of spring values in both libraries' formats (motion `stiffness`/`damping`, react-spring `tension`/`friction`).\n- Pattern: `springs.snappy | gentle | bouncy`, `durations.fast | base | slow`, `easeOut`.\n\n**Section primitive (`src/layout/Section.tsx`):**\n- Purpose: `<section id aria-labelledby>` with an `<h2>` heading and `Container`.\n- Pattern: props in (`id`, `title`, `className`, `children`), JSX out.\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser document:**\n- Location: `index.html`\n- Triggers: any navigation to the site root or `base` path.\n- Responsibilities: pre-paint theme selection, static shell, module script tag.\n\n**Module entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: `<script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\">` in `index.html`.\n- Responsibilities: `createRoot(#root).render(<StrictMode><App/></StrictMode>)`.\n\n**Build entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `npm run dev`, `npm run build`, `npm run preview`.\n- Responsibilities: base path, plugins, `@` alias.\n\n**Test entries:**\n- Unit: `vitest.config.ts` (`tests/unit/**`), setup `tests/setup.ts`.\n- E2E: `playwright.config.ts` (`tests/e2e/**`), local preview on port 4173 or the URL in `E2E_BASE_URL`.\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No workers. No server.\n- **Global state:** `document.documentElement` `data-theme` attribute and `localStorage` key `theme` are shared mutable state (`src/lib/theme.ts`). `Globals` from `@react-spring/web` is module-level state assigned in `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`.\n- **Circular imports:** None observed in `src/`.\n- **Lazy boundary:** `motion/react` text effects are split into their own chunk by `React.lazy` in `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx`. Keep the static path free of motion imports to protect the initial JS budget (150 kB gzipped, `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`).\n- **Import boundaries (enforced):** `.oxlintrc.json` `no-restricted-imports` blocks `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web` outside `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/`, blocks deep imports of `@/anim/**` (use the barrel), blocks `@/content` imports outside `src/app/Page.tsx`, and blocks `src/anim/` from importing `sections`, `layout` or `content`. Run `npm run lint` after changing imports.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing animation libraries from sections or UI\n\n**What happens:** A component imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** It bypasses the motion-preference wiring and the token table, and fails the lint boundary in `.oxlintrc.json`.\n**Do this instead:** Add a semantic wrapper in `src/anim/` and export it from `src/anim/index.ts`, as `TextReveal` and `ThemeThumb` do.\n\n### Animating the same element with both libraries\n\n**What happens:** A single element receives both a motion animation and a react-spring value.\n**Why it's wrong:** Both write `transform`; the outcomes fight and the reduced-motion handling becomes unclear.\n**Do this instead:** Pick one owner per interaction, following the ownership table in `docs/architecture.md` (motion for mount, exit, layout and scroll; react-spring for velocity physics).\n\n### Writing the theme attribute directly\n\n**What happens:** Code calls `document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', ...)` or writes `localStorage` outside `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Bypasses the storage-blocked fallback and the cross-tab sync logic in `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n**Do this instead:** Call `applyTheme(theme)` from `src/lib/theme.ts`. Read with `useTheme()` from `src/lib/useTheme.ts`.\n\n### Hardcoded asset paths\n\n**What happens:** Links to `/resume.pdf` or `/favicon.svg` are written without the base.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks when `VITE_BASE` changes from `/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/` to `/` at cutover.\n**Do this instead:** Use `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` as `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` does, or `%BASE_URL%` in `index.html`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Degrade to static content. The page never goes blank because a chunk failed.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `ChunkBoundary` class component in `src/anim/declarative/TextReveal.tsx` catches a failed lazy chunk load and renders the plain paragraph.\n- `Suspense` fallback in `TextReveal` renders the same paragraph while the chunk loads.\n- `localStorage` access and `matchMedia` calls are wrapped in try/catch in `src/lib/theme.ts` and in the inline script in `index.html`.\n- No global error boundary. No error reporting.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in runtime code.\n**Validation:** Build-time gates in `scripts/` (`check-colors.mjs`, `check-contrast.mjs`, `check-path-case.mjs`, `check-js-budget.mjs`, `check-no-phone.mjs`), run by `npm run verify`.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable.\n**Accessibility:** One `<main id=\"main\">`; `aria-labelledby` on sections; `aria-pressed` on the theme toggle; the `reveal-pending` class keeps text visible when motion is off.\n**Motion:** One preference source, `useMotionPreference`, feeds motion, react-spring and the CSS guard.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-20*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":869,"cache_read_input_tokens":99218,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":869,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":10,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01PKUbVN6a7b7xcgA76bM27t","agent_id":"a95394deb18337775","session_id":"cb24716b-4117-4f14-8b25-6e3939021776","uuid":"eaf40b0d-d6b6-4d6e-99cb-f0a6d778d9d2","timestamp":"2026-11-20T07:03:42.665Z","request_id":"req_011ChDkcMMi3CnqQ2M7TBdk2","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Map codebase tech and architecture"}

There is no API client, database, auth provider, analytics, or error-tracking SDK in `src/`.\n\nThe only external services are build-time and hosting services: the npm registry (packages), GitHub Actions (CI and deploy), and GitHub Pages (hosting).\n\n## APIs & External Services\n\n**Runtime third-party APIs:**\n- None.
